  try {
    if (window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone) return;
    window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone = true;

    var path = (window.location.pathname || '/').split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content')) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}

mb-14" data-page-title="Plans and limits" data-page-href="/guides/plans-and-limits" id="content"><span data-as="p">Lightdrift is pay-as-you-go. Your <strong>credit balance</strong> pays for searches, while your <strong>plan</strong> determines how many requests you can make and how many searches can run at once.</span>
<span data-as="p">Check your current plan, limits, and progress toward the next plan in <a href="https://lightdrift.ai/dashboard/plan" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Plan and limits</a>. Buy credit and view your balance in <a href="https://lightdrift.ai/dashboard/billing" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Credits</a>.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="plans-at-a-glance"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#plans-at-a-glance" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Plans at a glance</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Plans are based on your cumulative credit purchases in USD. These are purchase thresholds, not monthly subscription prices.</span>

<div role="list" class="steps ml-3.5 mt-10 mb-6"><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-white/10" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div data-component-part="step-indicator" class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="[[data-component-part=step-indicator]:has(a:focus-visible)_&amp;]:opacity-0">1</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Purchase search credit</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">Add credit in <a href="https://lightdrift.ai/dashboard/billing" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Credits</a>. Each credit purchase contributes to your cumulative purchase total.</span></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-white/10" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div data-component-part="step-indicator" class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="[[data-component-part=step-indicator]:has(a:focus-visible)_&amp;]:opacity-0">2</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Reach a plan threshold</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">Your plan upgrades automatically when the total reaches $10, $100, or $1,000. You do not pay a separate upgrade fee.</span></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-transparent bg-linear-to-b from-gray-200 dark:from-white/10 via-80% to-transparent [.step-container:has([data-component-part=step-content]:empty)_&amp;]:hidden" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div data-component-part="step-indicator" class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="[[data-component-part=step-indicator]:has(a:focus-visible)_&amp;]:opacity-0">3</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Use the credit with higher limits</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">The purchased credit remains available to pay for searches. Using your balance does not reduce your cumulative purchase total or lower your plan.</span></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Example:</strong> You have purchased $5 and are on Promo. Purchase another $5 to reach $10 in total purchases and unlock Starter: 60 requests per minute, 5,000 per day, and 4 concurrent searches. That new $5 is added to your search balance.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Note" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><path d="M 9 1.6714 C 13.0371 1.6714 16.3286 4.9629 16.3286 9 C 16.3286 13.0371 13.0371 16.3286 9 16.3286 C 7.0574 16.3252 5.1953 15.552 3.8217 14.1783 C 2.448 12.8047 1.6748 10.9426 1.6714 9 C 1.6714 4.9629 4.9629 1.6714 9 1.6714 Z M 9 0 C 4.0371 0 0 4.0371 0 9 C 0 13.9629 4.0371 18 9 18 C 13.9629 18 18 13.9629 18 9 C 18 4.0371 13.9629 0 9 0 Z M 10.2857 3.8571 H 7.7143 V 10.2857 H 10.2857 V 3.8571 Z M 10.2857 11.5714 H 7.7143 V 14.1429 H 10.2857 V 11.5714 Z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">Promotional credit and account adjustments do not count toward plan upgrades. Your available balance can therefore be different from the purchase total shown on your plan page.</span></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="what-each-limit-means"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#what-each-limit-means" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">What each limit means</span></h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>Requests per minute:</strong> how many authenticated requests you can make in a minute window.</li>
<li><strong>Requests per day:</strong> how many authenticated requests you can make in a daily window.</li>
<li><strong>Concurrent searches:</strong> how many searches can be in progress at the same time across your account. Once a search finishes, its slot becomes available again.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Minute and daily request limits use fixed windows. Requests count toward these limits even when rejected; repeatedly retrying will not bypass the limit. A request limit is not a billing charge: failed searches are not charged.</span>
<span data-as="p">With API keys, minute and daily counters are tracked per key. Images MCP connections authenticated through OAuth share a request counter for the account. Concurrent searches are counted across the account. Custom API-key limits may override the plan defaults.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="search-pricing-and-balance"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#search-pricing-and-balance" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Search pricing and balance</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">A successful image search or similar-image search costs <strong>$0.005</strong>. Retrieving image details is free. Higher plans increase request capacity; the request allowances do not include free searches.</span>
<span data-as="p">You need both available credit and room within your limits to run a paid search. A high plan does not replace a credit balance. See <a class="link" href="/guides/images-mcp">Images MCP</a> for tool pricing and <a class="link" href="/guides/search">the search guide</a> for REST usage.</span>
<span data-as="p"><a class="link" href="/guides/docs-mcp">Docs MCP</a> reads public documentation and does not spend Lightdrift search credit.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="when-a-request-is-blocked"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#when-a-request-is-blocked" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">When a request is blocked</span></h2>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_4sllktbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Response</th><th>Meaning</th><th>What to do</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td data-numeric="true"><code>402</code></td><td>Not enough credit for the search</td><td>Check your balance and <a href="https://lightdrift.ai/dashboard/billing" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">add credit</a>.</td></tr><tr><td><code>429</code> — request limit</td><td>Minute or daily allowance reached</td><td>Wait for the <code>Retry-After</code> delay before retrying. Pace requests instead of sending them in bursts.</td></tr><tr><td><code>429</code> — concurrent searches</td><td>Too many searches are already running</td><td>Wait for an active search to finish and respect <code>Retry-After</code>. Use a queue to limit parallel searches.</td></tr><tr><td data-numeric="true"><code>503</code></td><td>Search or a required provider is temporarily unavailable</td><td>Respect <code>Retry-After</code> and retry. Failed searches are not charged; there is no legacy GPU warm-up path.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="headers-for-api-integrations"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#headers-for-api-integrations" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Headers for API integrations</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Use the response headers to pace REST requests. Headers available depend on the response; a concurrency rejection, for example, includes <code>Retry-After</code> and <code>X-Concurrency-Limit</code>.</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_5kllktbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Header</th><th>Meaning</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>X-RateLimit-Tier</code></td><td>The effective plan for the request</td></tr><tr><td><code>X-RateLimit-Limit</code></td><td>The minute and daily request limits</td></tr><tr><td><code>X-RateLimit-Remaining</code></td><td>The smaller remaining allowance across the minute and daily windows</td></tr><tr><td><code>X-RateLimit-Reset</code></td><td>Seconds until the minute window resets; on a request-limit rejection, seconds until the blocking window resets</td></tr><tr><td><code>X-Concurrency-Limit</code></td><td>The effective limit for simultaneous searches</td></tr><tr><td><code>Retry-After</code></td><td>Seconds to wait before retrying a rejected request</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Your <a href="https://lightdrift.ai/dashboard/plan" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">dashboard plan page</a> shows the account’s plan defaults. API headers reflect the effective limits for a particular request, including custom key limits.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="search-price-and-existing-credits"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#search-price-and-existing-credits" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Search price and existing credits</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">A successful search costs <strong><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>0.005</mn><mo stretchy="false">(</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">0.005 (</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mord">0.005</span><span class="mopen">(</span></span></span></span>5 per 1,000)</strong>, including text, image, image-plus-text and similar-image queries. Standard reranking defaults to 60 candidates and supports at most 100; the playground uses 100. Metadata retrieval and Docs MCP are free. The public <code>/v1/pricing</code> endpoint reports the effective rate.</span>
<span data-as="p">Credits remain dollar balances: <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>20</mn><mi>f</mi><mi>u</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>s</mi><mn>4</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mn>000</mn><mi>s</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>r</mi><mi>c</mi><mi>h</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>s</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>d</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">20 funds 4,000 searches and </annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8889em;vertical-align:-0.1944em"></span><span class="mord">20</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10764em">f</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mord mathnormal">d</span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span><span class="mord">4</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em"></span><span class="mord">000</span><span class="mord mathnormal">se</span><span class="mord mathnormal">a</span><span class="mord mathnormal">rc</span><span class="mord mathnormal">h</span><span class="mord mathnormal">es</span><span class="mord mathnormal">an</span><span class="mord mathnormal">d</span></span></span></span>5 funds 1,000. Existing balances and historical charges are unchanged. The minimum top-up remains $5. Free playground allowances, dollar-valued signup grants and automatic refill amounts remain unchanged. Failed requests refund the amount originally charged, including requests spanning a price change. A failed and refunded request retried with its old idempotency key returns 409; use a new key to retry.</span></div><nav id="pagination" aria-label="Pagination" class="px-0.5 flex items-center gap-6
